- **Grayscale 4** [pass]: Desaturation as in Grayscale 1 (Color Balance (HLS) Saturation -100) followed by a Curves effect stored with a strong S-curve: darks below about 20% are crushed toward black and highlights above about 80% clip toward white, so it is a punchy, high-contrast black and white, clearly harder than Grayscale 1.
- **Hand Drawn Effect** [weak]: Hand-drawn 'boil': two Turbulent Displace effects ('Turbulent Displace - Low Freq': Size 50, Complexity 2; '- High Freq': Size 5, Complexity 5; both Pinning Pin All) wobble the layer, driven by four Slider Controls: 'Overall Displacement' (8, low-freq Amount), 'Rough Edges' (30, high-freq Amount), 'Effect Speed' (8, boil frame rate via posterizeTime) and 'Wiggle Amount' (1500, evolution speed). At these defaults the boil is subtle on footage: edges roughen and wobble by only a few pixels, easy to miss on soft or busy shots; it reads better on crisp line art and text, and needs higher 'Overall Displacement' / 'Rough Edges' for an obvious hand-drawn look.
- **Mood Lighting - amorphous** [pass]: Overlays animated greyscale Fractal Noise (Basic, Soft Linear, Contrast 225, Brightness -25) on the layer in Overlay mode, so soft drifting blobs brighten and darken the picture like shifting stage or window light (no colour of its own). The 'Mood Lighting - amorphous' controls drive it: 'Evolution Speed' 150, 'Cloud Size' 75 (noise Scale = layer width x Cloud Size / 50) and 'Intensity' 50 (noise Opacity).
- **Mood Lighting - digital** [pass]: Overlays animated greyscale Fractal Noise (Threads, Block noise, Contrast 170) on the layer in Overlay mode, so hard-edged rectangular light patches flicker and shift across the picture. The 'Mood Lighting - digital' controls drive it: 'Evolution Speed' 100, 'Block Size' 250 (noise Scale = layer width x Block Size / 500) and 'Intensity' 75 (noise Opacity).
- **Mood Lighting - streaks** [weak]: Overlays animated greyscale Fractal Noise (Threads, Soft Linear, Brightness 33) stretched vertically (Scale Height = layer height x 4) on the layer in Soft Light mode, so tall vertical light streaks drift across the picture. The 'Mood Lighting - streaks' controls drive it: 'Evolution Speed' 200, 'Streak Width' 75 (Scale Width = layer width x Streak Width / 400) and 'Intensity' 75 (noise Opacity). At the default Intensity 75 the streaks are soft on normal footage: the frame lifts about 5 levels on average and the strongest vertical band about 20, showing mostly as a pale haze over dark areas (Soft Light) and barely on bright walls, so raise 'Intensity' for clearly visible light streaks.
- **Noisy Vignette** [pass]: CC Vignette (Amount 100, Angle of View 45, centred) darkens the frame edges and Noise (Amount of Noise 8%, colour noise on) adds moving grain: a quick filmic edge falloff with texture.
- **VHS Flicker Effect** [pass]: VHS look: Venetian Blinds (Transition Completion 80, Width 7, Direction 90) cuts the picture into scan lines, Wave Warp (Square, Wave Height 4, Wave Width 84.5) adds horizontal line jitter, two Glows ('Glow 1' radius 20, 'Glow 2' radius 162, Composite Original Behind) bloom the lines back together, Fast Box Blur (0.3) softens, and Solid Composite's keyframed 'Source Opacity' flickers the layer through a looping, randomised expression.
- **Vignette Lighting** [pass]: Spotlight vignette: a Gradient Ramp named 'Ramp' draws a radial ramp from 55% grey (#8C8C8C) at the layer centre to black at the corners (expressions put Start at the centre and End at [0,0]), Levels is added at neutral values, and Calculations blends the original layer back in Hard Light, so the centre stays near normal and the edges fall off to dark.
- **Stacked Drop Shadows** *(needs_input)* [untested: needs_setup]: Three hard-edged Drop Shadows ('First/Second/Third Drop Shadow', Softness 0) in black, cyan (#00F0FF) and green (#00FF24), offset 13, 10 and 8 px in one shared direction; each shadow also copies the ones before it, so they compound into a stepped, multi-colour retro extrusion behind text or logos. Unverified: in the gallery render on full-frame footage all three shadows fell behind the opaque layer and nothing changed, so it needs a re-test on a text or shape layer before use.

### Image - Special Effects

- **Bad TV 1 - warp** [pass]: Strongest warp of the three Bad TV presets: Grow Bounds (60 px) lets the picture spill past the layer edge, Wave Warp (Semicircle, Wave Width = layer height, Wave Height = noise(time) * 60 + 30) bends it sideways, Transform shifts the whole picture horizontally by the same wave height, Color Balance (HLS) Saturation -33 mutes colour, Noise (20%, colour) adds static and Venetian Blinds (15%, Width 5) cuts scan lines.
- **Bad TV 2 - old** [pass]: Old, soft television: Wave Warp (Smooth Noise, Wave Height 50, Wave Width 600, Wave Speed -0.05) slowly sways the picture, Fast Box Blur (Blur Radius 4, Horizontal) smears it sideways, Color Balance (HLS) Saturation +33 over-saturates colour like an old tube set, Noise (20%, colour) adds static and Venetian Blinds (15%, Width 5) cuts scan lines.
- **Bad TV 3 - weak** [pass]: Weak-signal TV: the heaviest static of the set (Noise 33%, colour) and the most washed-out colour (Color Balance (HLS) Saturation -50), with a Wave Warp waver (Noise type, Wave Width 500, Wave Height = noise(time/1.5) * 50 + 25) and Venetian Blinds scan lines (15%, Width 5); no blur and no picture shift.
- **Cracked Tiles** [pass]: Card Wipe (Transition Completion 0) splits the layer into a tile grid set by the 'Cracked Tiles' pseudo-effect: 'Tiles Across' (50) sets both Rows and Columns, and 'Tile Cracking' (33) shrinks each card (Card Scale 1 to 0.9) to open thin cracks and drives X/Y/Z Position Jitter, so the picture looks like a cracked tile mosaic whose pieces drift slightly. No rotation.
- **Light Leaks - random** [pass]: Random exposure flicker: Calculations adds the layer's original pixels onto itself (Add) and Solid Composite adds white (Add), both at a strength set by a noise() expression from the 'Light Leaks - random' controls 'Chance of Flashing' (100) and 'Flash Nervousness' (50), so brightness surges up and down at random. A white overexposure flicker, not a coloured leak.
- **Motion Registration Errors** [pass]: Time Difference compares the layer with itself about 0.25 s away in time (Time Offset (sec) wiggles 0.25 +- 0.25 via wiggle(1, .25), Contrast 200, Absolute Difference off), and Calculations blends the original back in Hard Light, so static areas look normal while moving edges get light/dark, colour-fringed ghost outlines like misregistered film.
- **Night Vision** [pass]: Green night-vision: Solid Composite puts green (#248000) under the layer in Luminosity mode so the picture becomes green monochrome, Minimax (Maximum, Radius 1) slightly fattens highlights, Noise (10%, monochrome) adds sensor grain and Venetian Blinds (Transition Completion 10, Width = layer height / 100) cuts fine scan lines.
- **Light Leaks - layer markers** *(needs_input)* [untested: needs_setup]: White exposure flash on every layer marker: within 'Flash Width (msec)' (200 ms in total) of each marker, Calculations adds the layer's original pixels onto itself (Blending Mode Add, Second Layer Opacity eased up and back down by expression) and Solid Composite adds white (Add, Opacity = half that value), so the frame blows out toward white for a moment. It is a flash, not a coloured light-leak texture. Unverified: the gallery test layer had no markers and both frames were unchanged, so it needs a re-test with a marker at the sampled time before use.

### Image - Utilities

- **Alpha From Lightness (Unmult)** [pass]: Makes alpha from brightness: Shift Channels takes Alpha from Lightness (Red, Green, Blue unchanged) and Remove Color Matting un-premultiplies against black, so black areas go transparent. Because Lightness is (max+min)/2, any channel brighter than it overflows when un-premultiplied and is clipped in 8/16-bpc projects: saturated colours lose their strongest channel, so warm footage turns desaturated olive/greenish-grey (over black it shows as min(channel, lightness)). Clean colour needs a 32-bpc project.
- **Compress-Expand Dynamic Range** [pass]: Adds two HDR Compander effects back to back: the first with Mode Compress Range, the second with Mode Expand Range (Gain 1, Gamma 1 on both). Moving an 8- or 16-bit-only effect between them lets 32-bpc over-range highlights survive it; as applied, with nothing in between, the pair changes nothing.
- **Crop Edges** [pass]: Crops all four edges by one shared amount: the 'Crop Edges' pseudo-effect ('Crop Amount (per edge)' 5, 'Feather' 0) drives four Linear Wipes (Wipe Angle 0, 180, 90, -90) by expression, so 5% is cut from every side, leaving transparency.
- **Flip** [pass]: Flips the layer upside down (vertical mirror) with a Transform effect: Scale Height -100, Uniform Scale off, Anchor Point and Position expression-locked to the layer centre, so the layer's own transform is untouched.
- **Flip + Flop** [pass]: Transform effect with Uniform Scale on and Scale -100, centred on the layer by expressions (Anchor Point and Position = layer centre): the picture is mirrored on both axes at once, which equals rotating it 180 degrees (upside down). It is not a left-right mirror.
- **Flop** [pass]: Mirrors the layer left-right (horizontal flip) with a Transform effect: Scale Width -100, Uniform Scale off, Anchor Point and Position expression-locked to the layer centre, so the layer's own transform is untouched.
- **Invert Alpha** [pass]: Inverts the layer's transparency: a Levels pass sets the Alpha channel Output Black 1 / Output White 0, Set Matte re-applies the layer's own alpha, and a second Levels trims faint alpha (Alpha Input Black about 0.25). Formerly transparent areas become opaque - and black, because transparent pixels carry no colour.
- **Keying - blue blur** [weak]: Channel Blur on the blue channel only (Blue Blurriness 4, Blur Dimensions Horizontal, Repeat Edge Pixels on) to smooth the blocky, chroma-subsampled edges of DV / compressed blue-screen footage before keying; little visible change on its own; on ordinary footage the change is invisible (render differed from the source by well under 1/255 on average).
- **Keying - green blur** [weak]: Channel Blur on the green channel only (Green Blurriness 2, Blur Dimensions Horizontal, Repeat Edge Pixels on) to smooth the blocky, chroma-subsampled edges of DV / compressed green-screen footage before keying; little visible change on its own; on ordinary footage the change is invisible (render differed from the source by well under 1/255 on average).
- **Levels - Video to Computer** [pass]: Expands 16-235 video-range levels to full 0-255 (Levels RGB Input Black 16/255, Input White 235/255), deepening blacks and brightening whites.
- **Levels - computer to video** [pass]: Compresses full-range 0-255 levels into the 16-235 broadcast video range (Levels RGB Output Black 16/255, Output White 235/255), slightly lifting blacks and lowering whites.
- **Magnify & Preserve Alpha** [pass]: Magnifying loupe that keeps the rest of the layer: a Magnify effect named '(Magnify)' (Circle, Magnification 200, Size 250, Link None, Blending Mode None, Center at the layer centre) is limited through its Compositing Options to the Add-mode mask 'Magnify Mask', whose path, feather and opacity expressions follow the lens. Outside that mask the layer stays untouched and visible, so no duplicate is needed underneath. The mask path is built with fromComp(Center + offset), i.e. it treats the effect's layer-space Center as comp coordinates: the mask only sits on the lens when the layer's pixels line up with the comp (100% scale, centred). On a scaled layer the mask drifts and shows a black/transparent hole instead of the loupe.
- **Keylight + Key Cleaner + Advanced Spill Suppressor** *(needs_input)* [pass]: Chroma-key stack: Keylight (1.2) with View set to Intermediate Result (keyed but not despilled), Key Cleaner (Additional Edge Radius 10, Strength 100) to recover edge detail, then Advanced Spill Suppressor (Standard method, Suppression 100) to remove screen-colour spill. Keylight's Screen Colour ships unset (black), so nothing is keyed until it is picked.
- **Sample Image Expression** *(needs_input)* [untested: needs_setup]: Rig utility: adds a 'Sample Image' pseudo-effect (Center, Radius, Sampled Color Output) whose colour output is the expression sampleImage(Center, [Radius, Radius]) on this layer - the average colour at a point (default Radius 0.5 = one pixel). No visible change by itself.

### Legacy

- **3D Lines Zoom In** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation, line by line: each line fades in from 300 px toward the camera (Position 3D z -300, Opacity 0) and settles into place while the line spacing closes from +60 px to normal; per-character 3D, range selector based on Lines (Ramp Up, offset -25 -> 100). -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **3D Rotate around Circle** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text carousel: puts the text on a circular mask (about 384 px across, mode None) with Perpendicular To Path and Reverse Path on, stands every character up (character X Rotation +90 on a layer with X Rotation -90) and animates First Margin 282 -> -602, so the letters travel about three-quarters of the way round a ring seen edge-on in perspective. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector; Text Path Options with a circular mask
- **Bars Blinky** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation: a wandering 35% band of random characters (Randomize Order, Smoothness 0) is stretched into faint full-height vertical bars (Scale [-300, 10000], Opacity 10%, Position x +50); the band's Offset runs wiggle(), so bars blink on and off continuously. Speed comes from the 'Wiggle_frequency' slider (default 8 = 0.8 wiggles/s; the expression divides by 10). -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Blue Flash** *(obsolete)* [broken]: Text animation: characters appear in random order, each dropping from 1000% scale and 0% opacity to normal size with a thick (Stroke Width 100) blue #004DFF stroke that thins away as it lands; Ramp Up selector based on Characters Excluding Spaces, offset -10 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Boxes - concentric NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN): five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - five concentric boxes from the centre outward: the four inner ones, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (648x486) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - concentric PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN): five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - five concentric boxes from the centre outward: the four inner ones, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (768x576) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 1 NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 1: five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - four scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (648x486) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 1 PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 1: five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - four scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (768x576) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 2 NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 2: five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - four scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (648x486) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 2 PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 2: five Add-mode masks fade the layer up - four scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 20-30, 40-50 and 60-70, then the full outer box (768x576) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 3 NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 3: six Add-mode masks fade the layer up - five scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 15-25, 30-40, 45-55 and 60-70, then the full outer box (648x486) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - random 3 PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Box reveal (transition IN), scattered-blocks variant 3: six Add-mode masks fade the layer up - five scattered boxes, each to 75% at completion 0-10, 15-25, 30-40, 45-55 and 60-70, then the full outer box (768x576) eases to 100% at 80-100 - driven by a 'Mask Fade Controls' Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s. -> Presets/Transitions - Wipes, or Linear/Venetian Blinds/Gradient Wipe effects on a 1920x1080 layer
- **Boxes - stroked NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Four scattered rectangles build on one after another: each Add-mode mask fades the layer in inside its box (completion 0-30, 23.3-53.3, 46.7-76.7, 70-100) while a Stroke effect (white, 1 px, All Masks, Stroke Sequentially) traces their outlines; driven by 'Fade Master Control' keyed 0 -> 100 over 2 s. The boxes sit in an SD-sized area (about 565x365 px). -> Shape-layer rectangles with Trim Paths, or Presets/Transitions - Wipes
- **Boxes - stroked PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Four scattered rectangles build on one after another: each Add-mode mask fades the layer in inside its box (completion 0-30, 23.3-53.3, 46.7-76.7, 70-100) while a Stroke effect (white, 1 px, All Masks, Stroke Sequentially) traces their outlines; driven by 'Fade Master Control' keyed 0 -> 100 over 2 s. The boxes sit in an SD-sized area (about 670x430 px). -> Shape-layer rectangles with Trim Paths, or Presets/Transitions - Wipes
- **Centipede** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text-on-path crawl: replaces the text with the word 'centipede' (Myriad Pro Bold), lays it on a long wavy mask 'Centipede Path' (about 3,500 px, Perpendicular To Path on) and animates First Margin 1462 -> 4548 over 4 s (slowing in the last 0.8 s) while the path itself shifts, so the letters crawl along the wave. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector; Text Path Options with an animated First Margin
- **Contract - Expand** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Demo text animation: replaces the text with five lines of 'contract' (Myriad Pro Regular) whose Source Text keyframes (0-1.8 s) switch each line to 'expand' in turn, while a Smooth range selector based on Lines (offset -50 -> 100, 0.67-2.17 s) shrinks each line to 0% scale at 50% opacity and grows it back as its word changes. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **DialTone-US** *(obsolete)* [untested: audio_only]: Audio: a Tone effect generating the US dial tone - sine waves at 350 Hz + 440 Hz mixed, Level 20%. -> Apply the Tone effect directly (audio effect)
- **Digital** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Animated background: Fractal Noise (Threads type, Soft Linear, Complexity 20) posterized to 2 levels, glowed, tinted by Tritone to green-on-black (shadows black, midtones #00D500, highlights #FAFFD5) and broken into 100 x 10 Mosaic blocks - a flickering green digital data / barcode texture. -> Presets/Backgrounds, or Fractal Noise + Mosaic + Tritone built directly
- **Drop Bounce** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation: characters land in random order by SCALE, not position - each starts at 2000% and 0% opacity, shrinks to normal size, then pops up again in smaller bounces (selectors 'Fall', 'Bounce 1-3' with 100/50/10/1% amounts, Round shape) before the 'Hold' selector settles it. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Embossed Multiple Exposure** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation: characters grow in left to right from 0% scale (Ramp Up selector, offset -100 -> 100 over 2 s, ease 50), brightening from black through grey to their own fill as they reach full size. On text without a stroke that is all it does: the animator's black fill / white 50 px stroke produced no outline or echo in the gallery render (with a stroke applied the outline is untested). -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Emerge** *(obsolete)* [untested: needs_setup]: Text animation: characters in random order start with a heavy 12 px stroke that thins back to the text's own stroke (Ramp Up, offset -100 -> 100 over 3 s, ease 25/25), so letters emerge from bold blobs into clean type. Only the stroke width animates - no fade or move. On a text layer without a stroke it renders no change at all (gallery render). -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Ethereal** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Animated background: a Wave World height map (two wave producers, keyed for 5 s) smoothed by Median (radius 20) and tinted by Tritone to soft blue (midtones #3699C9, white highlights, black shadows) - a calm, rippling liquid-light texture. -> Presets/Backgrounds, or Wave World + Tritone built directly
- **Fluctuate** *(obsolete)* [weak]: Text animation (continuous): a Wiggly Selector (0.5 Hz, correlation 100) intersected with a random selector swells the characters' stroke width up to 150 while fading stroke opacity, so the outline weight pulses slowly; a second animator ('Hide Last Char') fills the last ~10% of characters black. On a text layer without a stroke only the black last character shows (gallery render). -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector with a Wiggly Selector
- **Fly to Inset** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Picture-in-picture transition: a 'Fly to Inset' control (Transition Completion keyed 0 -> 100 over 1 s) drives a Transform effect that shrinks the layer to 33.3% and moves it to the Target Position; four Linear Wipes crop 5% off each edge and a Vegas frame (8 px, #E5E5E5), Bevel Alpha and Drop Shadow (67%) ramp on. Rendered on 1920x1080 footage the inset flew to the UPPER-LEFT (centre about [470, 128] layer px) with its top edge clipped by the top of the layer: the stored Target Position (fractions 0.733, 0.267) resolved as if on a 640x480 frame, not to the intended upper right. Set Target Position yourself after applying. -> Animate Scale/Position (or the Transform effect) to a corner box with Drop Shadow and Bevel Alpha
- **Frenzy** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text-on-path: replaces the text with the word 'frenzy' (Myriad Pro Bold), lays it on a fixed zig-zag mask ('Zig-Zag Path', 243 points, SD-sized) and drives First Margin from -8780 to 11734 in 2 s, so the word races back and forth along the zig-zag. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector; Text Path Options with an animated mask path
- **Front - Back** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Demo text animation: replaces the text with five lines of 'front' (Myriad Pro Bold) whose Source Text keyframes (0-1.8 s) switch each line to 'back' in turn, while a Smooth range selector based on Lines (offset -50 -> 100, 0.67-2.17 s) squashes each line's height to 0 at 50% opacity and back - a split-flap / card-turn word swap. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Frontside - Backside** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Demo text animation: replaces the text with five lines of 'frontside' (Myriad Pro Bold) whose Source Text keyframes (0-1.7 s) switch each line to 'backside' in turn, while a Smooth character-based selector (offset -100 -> 100, 0.67-2.17 s, tracking -42) squeezes each character's width to 0 at 50% opacity and back - letters turn like cards on a vertical axis. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Green Dots** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation (continuous): a randomized 40% range wanders over the text via wiggle() (speed from the 'Wiggle Frequency' slider, default 1). In bursts, the characters it selects drop out of the word and large tilted translucent green squares (roughly 150-250 px across with 75 px text; the Character Value 183 middle-dot replacements drawn very large at low opacity, hue-shifted) flash over the text while the remaining letters tint pale green; a second animator hides some characters. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector with Character Value and a Wiggly Selector
- **Incoming** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation, line by line: each line slides in from 650 px to the right (Ramp Up selector based on Lines, offset -46.7 -> 100 over 2 s) while a third animator unsqueezes the characters from 7% width with -36 tracking to normal (0.83-2.0 s). Nothing fades: lines are visible while offset. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector
- **Inflammation** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text animation: gives all characters a red #FF1212 stroke, then a Wiggly Selector (Lock Dimensions, correlation 95) shrinks characters and swells their stroke width (up to 100) in a wave that travels through the text as Spatial Phase is keyed 2520 -> 0 over 5 s; temporal frequency is 0, so the motion stops after 5 s. -> Presets/Text (Animate In / Animate Out / 3D Text categories) or a hand-built Text Animator with a Range Selector with a Wiggly Selector
- **Inset Video - framed** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Turns the layer into a picture-in-picture inset: scales and positions it, crops the edges with four Linear Wipes, draws a coloured frame around it (Vegas), then adds a bevel and a drop shadow. An 'Inset Video - framed' control effect drives Crop Amount, Frame Size and Frame Color through expressions. -> Scale the layer down and add Layer Styles (Stroke, Bevel and Emboss, Drop Shadow) or the Drop Shadow / Bevel Alpha effects directly; crop with a mask or Linear Wipe
- **Inset Video - torn edges** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Picture-in-picture inset with ragged, torn-paper edges: scales and positions the layer, crops it with four Linear Wipes, roughens the border with Roughen Edges and adds a drop shadow. A control effect sets the crop amount. -> Roughen Edges + Drop Shadow effects on a scaled layer, with a mask or Linear Wipe for the crop
- **Left Third - scoop mask NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Adds four overlapping, partly transparent elliptical masks that cut the layer down to a tall vertical oval with soft, stepped edges; everything outside goes transparent. Applied to a 1920x1080 layer the shape sits in centre-right of the frame (about 37-75% of the width), running from just below the top to the bottom edge, not in the left third: the mask vertices were drawn for an SD frame. The mask paths are keyframed to morph very slowly (tens of seconds), so in a short shot the shape is static. -> Draw the panel as a shape layer or Pen-tool mask (or use a Premiere Essential Graphics panel); there is no modern preset
- **Left Third - scoop mask PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Adds four overlapping, partly transparent elliptical masks that cut the layer down to a tall vertical oval with soft, stepped edges; everything outside goes transparent. Applied to a 1920x1080 layer the shape sits in the centre of the frame (about 35-80% of the width), full height, not in the left third: the mask vertices were drawn for an SD frame. The mask paths are keyframed to morph very slowly (tens of seconds), so in a short shot the shape is static. -> Draw the panel as a shape layer or Pen-tool mask (or use a Premiere Essential Graphics panel); there is no modern preset
- **Lightning - Horizontal** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Hides the layer's own picture (Composite on Original is off; it renders as black over the black test comp) and draws a green, glowing Advanced Lightning bolt that arcs horizontally from the left edge about a third of the way across, near the top of a 1920x1080 layer (the origin/direction points were set for an SD frame). Conductivity State is keyframed so the bolt crackles and changes shape. -> Advanced Lightning effect applied directly (match name ADBE Lightning 2)
- **Lightning - Vertical** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Applies Advanced Lightning striking vertically, with Origin, Conductivity State, Core Radius, Turbulence, Forking and Decay all keyframed, so the bolt moves, thickens and branches as it plays. -> Advanced Lightning effect applied directly (match name ADBE Lightning 2)
- **Lower Third - scoop mask NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Adds four overlapping, partly transparent elliptical masks that cut the layer down to a wide horizontal oval with soft, stepped edges; everything outside goes transparent. Applied to a 1920x1080 layer the shape sits in the upper-middle of the frame (about 21-79% of the width, 8-67% of the height), not in the lower third: the mask vertices were drawn for an SD frame. The mask paths are keyframed to morph very slowly (tens of seconds), so in a short shot the shape is static. -> Draw the panel as a shape layer or Pen-tool mask (or use a Premiere Essential Graphics panel); there is no modern preset
- **Lower Third - scoop mask PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Adds four overlapping, partly transparent elliptical masks that cut the layer down to a wide horizontal oval with soft, stepped edges; everything outside goes transparent. Applied to a 1920x1080 layer the shape sits in the upper-middle of the frame (about 16-84% of the width, top to 70% of the height), not in the lower third: the mask vertices were drawn for an SD frame. The mask paths are keyframed to morph very slowly (tens of seconds), so in a short shot the shape is static. -> Draw the panel as a shape layer or Pen-tool mask (or use a Premiere Essential Graphics panel); there is no modern preset
- **Lower Third Holdout - darken** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Darkens the bottom of the layer with a soft gradient: Gradient Ramp runs from 75% to 85% of the layer height (set by expressions) and Calculations blends it back over the footage, giving a dark band so lower-third text reads. -> Gradient Ramp (or a Linear Gradient shape) on a solid or adjustment layer in Multiply mode
- **Lower Third Holdout - saturate** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Same gradient holdout as the darken version (Gradient Ramp at 75-85% of the layer height, blended back over the footage by Calculations), but the ramp ends in black instead of dark grey and Calculations uses Hard Light instead of Overlay (blend-mode indices decoded from the .ffx, look not checked in AE), so the lower band gets harsher contrast and stronger colour rather than a plain darkening. -> Gradient Ramp on an adjustment layer with a Saturation/Color blend mode, or a masked Hue/Saturation adjustment layer
- **Lyrical** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Puts the text on a curvy mask path ('Lyrical Path') and flows it along with line-by-line Range Selector offsets, using X-position/scale, Y-position and scale animators for a flowing, wave-like lyric movement. -> Text > Paths presets (e.g. Serpent, Rope Bridge) or Text > Organic
- **Multi-Line Flip** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Multi-line text where one animator with a Range Selector sweeps through the text, flipping lines and characters in sequence. -> Text > 3D Text > 3D Flip In Rotate X, or the Text > Multi-Line presets
- **Organism** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text on a mask path ('Organism Path') that grows organically: characters scale up, First Margin slides the text along the curve, and scale/tracking animators make the letters breathe as they settle. -> Text > Organic or Text > Paths presets
- **Outgoing - Incoming** *(obsolete)* [pass]: An Expression Selector alternates every other line (textIndex % 2, based on lines): the preset's own five stacked 'outgoing' lines slide off sideways one after another, neighbouring lines in opposite directions, and an 'incoming' line slides in to replace each, cascading top to bottom like a headline swap. -> Text > Animate In > Alternating Characters In with Text > Animate Out > Alternating Characters Out
- **Ovals - concentric NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small concentric ovals fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 648x486) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 648x486 window of the layer visible, not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Ovals - concentric PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small concentric ovals fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 768x576) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 768x576 window of the layer visible, not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Ovals - random 1 NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small ovals at scattered positions fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 648x486) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 648x486 window of the layer visible (plus one or two of the earlier ovals still poking out past its edges), not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Ovals - random 1 PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small ovals at scattered positions fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 768x576) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 768x576 window of the layer visible (plus one or two of the earlier ovals still poking out past its edges), not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Ovals - random 2 NTSC** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small ovals at scattered positions fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 648x486) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 648x486 window of the layer visible (plus one or two of the earlier ovals still poking out past its edges), not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Ovals - random 2 PAL** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Transition reveal: small ovals at scattered positions fade on one after another in the middle of the layer, then the final mask - a hard-edged rectangle the size of a square-pixel SD frame (about 768x576) centred on the layer - fades to full opacity. On a 1920x1080 layer the transition therefore ends with only a centred 768x576 window of the layer visible (plus one or two of the earlier ovals still poking out past its edges), not the full frame; scale the last mask up (about 250-300%) or work in an SD-sized precomp. A 'Mask Fade Controls' effect drives it: Transition Completion is keyframed 0 to 100 and Feather sets the mask feather. -> Iris Wipe effect, or the Transitions - Wipes > Inky Iris Wipe preset
- **Paper Clip** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text on a looping, paper-clip-shaped mask path: First Margin carries the text around the loops while scale and tracking animators (with a ramp-down selector) shrink and tighten the characters as they travel. -> Text > Paths presets (e.g. Loop In and Out, 360 Loop)
- **Pinwheel** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Characters orbit and rotate like a pinwheel: a looping position/rotation animator, a Y-scale animator and a position adjustment are swept across the text by an offset selector. -> Text > Curves and Spins presets (e.g. Spiral In, Whirl In)
- **Pulse Exposure** *(obsolete)* [pass]: A fill and stroke colour animator driven by a Wiggly Selector makes characters randomly pulse and flicker in brightness or colour, continuously. -> Text > Lights and Optical > Flicker Exposure, or Text > Fill and Stroke > Flicker Color
- **Reduce DV blockiness** *(obsolete)* [weak]: Converts the layer to YUV with Channel Combiner, blurs only the colour (U/V) channels with Channel Blur, then converts back to RGB, smoothing the blocky chroma of DV footage while keeping detail sharp. On clean HD footage the result is invisible (render test: at most 7/255 change per RGB channel, under 1/255 on average); it only shows on footage that actually has blocky DV/low-bitrate colour. -> No modern built-in chroma-only fix: rebuild it with Channel Combiner (RGB to YUV) + Channel Blur + Channel Combiner (YUV to RGB), or use Remove Grain in AE for general noise; Premiere has no equivalent effect
- **Separate XYZ Position** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Utility with no visible look: adds a 'Separate XYZ Position' control effect with X, Y and Z Position sliders and a Position expression (value + [X, Y, Z]), so each axis can be keyframed and eased separately. -> Built-in Separate Dimensions (right-click Position > Separate Dimensions; in script set the Position property's dimensionsSeparated = true)
- **Shadows** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Characters build on with jittery stroke width and tracking: an offset selector sweeps the text while a wiggle expression on the selector start makes the outline and spacing flicker, and the last character stays hidden until the end. -> Text > Fill and Stroke > Pulsing Strokes or Wiggly Stroke Width By Line
- **Silhouettes** *(obsolete)* [weak]: A stroke animator with random and wiggly selectors switches characters between filled and outline-only (silhouette) at random, while the last character stays hidden. -> Text > Fill and Stroke > Pulsing Strokes, or Text > Lights and Optical > Flicker Exposure
- **Sonar Ping** *(obsolete)* [pass]: An Expression Selector ((textIndex + time * Frequency) % 30) sends a repeating ramp of opacity and stroke across the characters, so a pulse sweeps through the text again and again like a sonar ping. A 'Frequency' slider sets the speed. -> Text > Fill and Stroke > Chasing Stroke
- **Spin Flash** *(obsolete)* [pass]: A scale, rotation and opacity animator swept by character: each letter spins and flashes into place in turn. -> Text > Rotation > Spin In
- **Spirograph** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Characters swing around offset anchor points in looping circular orbits while rotating and fading, tracing spirograph-like curves before settling into the word. -> Text > Curves and Spins > Spiral In or Text > Rotation > Swirly Rotation
- **Twisty Ribbons** *(obsolete)* [broken]: One animator with a shaped, eased Range Selector rolls a rotation and scale wave through the characters, so the line twists like a ribbon as the wave passes. -> Text > Rotation > Flip Up or Text > 3D Text presets
- **Twisty Ribbons 2** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Two animators flip characters around the Y axis and then the X axis in rolling waves, so the text twists like a ribbon. An expression keeps the X-flip selector's random order in sync with the Y-flip one. The text starts with every character flipped upside down, and they turn upright in random order. -> Text > 3D Text > 3D Flip In Rotate X, or Text > Rotation > Flip Up
- **Walk Of Stars** *(obsolete)* [pass]: Text on a star-shaped mask path whose First Margin is keyframed, so the words travel round the star. -> Text > Paths > Conveyor Belt
- **White Trim** *(obsolete)* [weak]: Characters jitter into place with a wiggling position and rotation while scale and opacity settle and a stroke-width animator trims the outline, giving a twitchy white-outlined build. -> Text > Fill and Stroke > Stroke Ease Down
- **Word Flash** *(obsolete)* [pass]: A scale, rotation and opacity animator swept by word: each word flashes and pops on one after another. -> Text > Animate In > Pop Buzz Words or Fade Up Words

### Shapes/Backgrounds

- **Box Swarm** [pass]: Creates a shape layer of about ten groups of rounded rectangles that drift, wiggle and change size, each coloured at random between two colours, as an abstract animated background.
- **Composition Border** [pass]: Creates a shape layer with a rectangle sized to the comp (expressions cancel out the layer's scale) and a stroke, drawing a solid-colour border around the frame edge. Controls set the width in pixels, the offset and the colour.
- **Kaleidoscopic** [pass]: Creates a shape layer holding one large filled-and-stroked polystar bent by Pucker & Bloat; expressions wiggle its radii, roundness and pucker amount, so the star/flower pattern keeps morphing like a kaleidoscope. All controls sit on an 'Animated Shape Control' effect.
- **Nerve Net - Circular** [pass]: Creates a shape layer with one stroked (unfilled) polystar bent by Pucker & Bloat into looping circular strands; wiggle expressions keep its points, radii and bloat drifting and pulsing, and Radial Blur plus Calculations on the layer smear the strokes into a glowing, nerve-like web.
- **Nerve Net - Penta** [pass]: Creates a shape layer with one stroked pentagon-style polystar bent by Pucker & Bloat and multiplied by a Repeater; wiggle expressions keep the copies shifting, and Fast Box Blur plus Calculations soften and blend them into a glowing geometric web.
- **Sunburst** [pass]: Radial sunburst: a disc of alternating Foreground Color rays clipped to Outer Radius, spinning at Rotation Speed, with an optional Background Color fill and an optional feathered hole in the middle (Inner Radius).

### Shapes/Elements

- **2D Text Box (Shape)** [pass]: Rectangle plate (square corners by default; Roundness rounds them, per corner if wanted) that auto-sizes to a chosen text layer's bounds, with padding, offset and scaling, and updates when the text changes. Default look: mid-grey fill with a white stroke.
- **Analogue Clock** [pass]: Flat white clock face (60 small minute dots, 12 larger hour dots) with tapered hour and minute hands driven by an angle control; the hands sweep and can loop forever.
- **Bar Graph** [pass]: Animated bar chart: X/Y axes grow in, then up to 12 bars grow up in a stagger; each bar height maps its Data slider between Floor and Ceiling to 0-500 px.
- **Bouncing Map Icons** [pass]: Map marker icon (six styles: three map markers, a push pin and two arrows) in one flat colour with a Bevel Alpha edge, bobbing up and down on a continuous sine wave.
- **Bounding Box** [pass]: Design-tool style selection box: a thin rectangle with eight square handles that can fit itself around a reference layer's bounds.
- **Burst Pop** [pass]: Cartoon 'pop' accent: a ring of stroked line particles shoots out from the centre and trims away, with optional gravity, twist and randomised spacing.
- **Chasing Line - Boxes** [pass]: A short train of boxes (Elements copies, Spread px apart; purple with a white outline by default) that marches across the full comp width in discrete steps and wraps around, like an LED chaser or loading strip.
- **Crinkle Cut Sticker** [pass]: Sticker-style frame with wavy crinkle-cut edges inside and out (smooth zig-zag, not sharp pinking): an outer rectangle minus an inset rectangle, each with a Zig Zag, that can wrap a reference layer. Default: purple fill with a white outline.
- **DNA Helix** [pass]: Animated double helix of dots joined by rungs that twists continuously, coloured by a Gradient Ramp run through Colorama.
- **Grid of Crosses** [pass]: Static grid of plus-sign crosses built with two repeaters, with size, thickness, rotation, colour, counts and gaps on controls.
- **Line Graph** [pass]: Animated line chart: axes grow in, then a line through up to 12 data points draws on left to right; point heights map Data values between Data Floor and Data Ceiling to Chart Height.
- **Marquee Chasers** [pass]: A 600x300 px rectangle outline (20 px stroke, 40/40 dashes) whose dashes crawl around the edge continuously (marching ants / chaser lights).
- **Popping Map Pin** [pass]: Map pin (five selectable styles: Arrow, Drop, Flag, X, Pin; the default Pin is a red ball on a white stick) that pops on with a scale overshoot and a short rotational wobble.
- **Postage Stamp** [pass]: Postage-stamp frame: a border with semicircular perforation cut-outs along every edge that can wrap a reference layer.
- **Radial Graph** [pass]: Donut chart with up to 10 ring segments, each sized to its share of the total, sweeping on one after another.
- **Repeating Lines** [pass]: Static set of parallel bars repeated side by side and centred on the layer, with width, length, count, gap, overall rotation and colour on controls; Skew Lines shifts each copy vertically for a staircase stagger.
- **Ring Chart** [pass]: Decorative HUD element: five concentric ring arcs whose Trim Start/End and offset wiggle randomly, each stroked in a random blend of two colours, with a Bevel Alpha edge. Not driven by data.
- **Scalloped Frame** [pass]: Ornamental frame with concave circular cut-outs (scallops) at each corner and at the middle of each side, an outer rectangle minus an inset rectangle, that can wrap a reference layer like a ticket or badge border.
- **Simple Map Marker** [pass]: Circular map dot with an outer ring that pulses outward on a loop, like a radar ping.
- **Switchable Cursor Element** [pass]: Static mouse-cursor icon picked from ten shapes by a dropdown: arrow cursor, pointer hand, open hand, hour glass, text I-beam and five resize/move arrows.
- **Wireframe Worm** [pass]: Repeated round-cornered rectangles (purple fill with a light outline by default) whose positions, scales and rotations wiggle, forming a writhing worm or tunnel.
- **Write On Arrow** [weak]: Stroked arrow that draws itself along a curved path while the arrowhead rides the tip and turns with the path. Default: white stroke; the head is a black triangle with a white outline.

### Shapes/Sprites - Animated

- **Alien Face - animated** [pass]: Abstract polystar glyph (lime-green fill, light outline by default) whose inner/outer radius and roundness wiggle continuously, so it morphs like a blobby alien face.
- **Circular - cellular division** [pass]: Dashed circle outline (thick yellow round-capped stroke by default) whose dash and gap lengths wiggle, so the segments split and merge like dividing cells (at default settings the first 1.6 s show a single arc growing, shrinking and turning), while the ring slowly drifts in rotation.
- **Dancing Knot** [pass]: Stroked star outline (thin light-green line by default) twisted by two Pucker & Bloat operators whose amounts and rotation wiggle, so it knots and unknots continuously.
- **Mandala - animated** [pass]: Filled and stroked polystar (orange fill, white stroke by default) whose radii and roundness wiggle, producing a continuously morphing mandala flower.
- **Phase Scope** [pass]: Thin light-grey stroked circles copied by a repeater and jittered by a Wiggle Transform, giving a shimmering oscilloscope-like trace. Not audio reactive.
- **SpiroMandala** [pass]: Two rectangles (thin white strokes by default) spun through a repeater with wiggled size and rotation, drawing a rotating spirograph-like mandala.
- **TwinkleStar** [pass]: An 8-point and a 4-point star layered (thin white rays fading toward the tips by default), whose outer radius (size) and rotation wiggle so the star twinkles.
- **Vintage Star** [pass]: Four-point sparkle made of two 4-sided polygons merged and rounded (Round Corners), whose scale stretches tall and thin on a sine wave: a retro twinkle.

### Shapes/Sprites - Still

- **Alien Calligraphy** [pass]: Static abstract stroke glyph: a trimmed, pucker-bloated polystar line (thin light-green stroke by default) that looks like an alien calligraphy mark.
- **Alien Face** [pass]: Static abstract glyph: a 3-point polystar with 1000 % roundness and an inner radius larger than the outer, giving a looping three-lobed blob, filled lime green with a light stroke.
- **Box - dashed lines** [pass]: Static 100x100 px square with a white 10/10 dashed outline and a blue fill at 25 % opacity.
- **Bullseye** [pass]: Static target: seven concentric circles (25 to 175 px) under one fill with the Even-Odd fill rule, so rings alternate filled and empty in a single colour.
- **Circular - half round** [pass]: Static thick semicircular arc: a 100 px circle stroked 33 px wide (white, round caps) and trimmed to 50 %.
- **Circular - tri dash** [pass]: Static 100 px ring: a 10 px white stroke dashed into three round-capped arcs (dash 50, gap 55). It also carries a lime-green 50 % opacity Fill 1 that is switched off in the preset, so only the arcs render.
- **Crosshair - brackets** [pass]: Static viewfinder brackets: a square frame whose side middles are cut away, leaving four corner brackets.
- **Crosshair - round** [pass]: Static round reticle: a circle (grey fill, white outline) with two thin cross lines extending past it (1.5x its size).
- **Crosshair - square** [pass]: Static square reticle: a square (grey fill, white outline) with two thin cross lines extending past it (1.5x its size).
- **Fan Blades** [pass]: A five-blade fan / propeller: a 5-point Polystar with 200% outer roundness (curved blades), white fill and 5 px grey stroke, with a 25 px centre hole cut by Merge Paths (Subtract).
- **Flower Power** [pass]: A retro flat flower: an 8-point Polystar with very high inner/outer roundness (wavy petals) plus a 50 px centre circle, yellow fill with a 6 px green stroke.
- **Gear 1** [pass]: A yellow 8-tooth cog with a 2 px white stroke: two 8-point stars intersected into square-ish teeth, merged with a round body, and a 61 px centre hole cut by Merge Paths (Subtract).
- **Gear 2** [pass]: A violet 10-point star (inner radius 50, outer 100) with a thick 20 px white bevel-joined stroke: reads as a spiky sprocket / saw-blade or seal badge. No hub or centre hole, and the teeth are pointed, not cog-shaped.
- **Holy Light** [pass]: A soft four-pointed glint: a 4-point Polystar pinched by Pucker & Bloat (-121) into sharp spikes, yellow fill at 50% opacity with a lavender stroke, slightly smeared by Radial Blur in Spin mode (Amount 10, centre kept on the layer anchor by expression). A sparkle/twinkle, not a god-ray burst.
- **Indian Sun** [pass]: A sun symbol: a 24-point Polystar (inner radius 37, outer 100, inner roundness 200 so the ray bases loop) around a 66 px centre disc, orange-yellow fill with a salmon stroke. The origin of the name is not documented.
- **Mandala** [pass]: A single 9-point Polystar with extreme roundness (inner 500%, outer 2000%, inner radius larger than outer) that loops back on itself into an overlapping rosette; orange fill, 3 px white stroke. One path, not separate petal and ring shapes.
- **RayStar-4** [pass]: A thin four-ray cross/sparkle: a needle-thin 4-point Polystar (inner radius 1, outer 100) with a white fill. Its 20 px white stroke is stored switched off (eye off), so in the render the rays are tapering hairlines about 2 px wide and 100 px long, not a bold plus. For a heavier star switch that Stroke on (should give about 20 px rays; not rendered) or raise the Polystar Inner Radius.
- **RayStar-8** [pass]: A thin eight-ray asterisk/spark: a needle-thin 8-point Polystar (inner radius 2, outer 100) with a white fill. Its 20 px white stroke is stored switched off (eye off), so in the render each ray is a tapering hairline about 2 px wide and 100 px long. For a bold spark switch that Stroke on (should give about 20 px spokes; not rendered) or raise the Polystar Inner Radius.
- **Rounded Bracket - beveled** [pass]: One rounded corner bracket (decoded: two 100 px rounded rectangles offset by 10 px, the second subtracted by Merge Paths, leaving an L-shaped sliver 10 px thick along the top and left edges, arms about 95 px; blue #0054FF fill, Bevel Alpha embossing; its 2 px white stroke is stored switched off). Rendered as a bright-blue embossed L-bracket whose rounded corner sits about 55 px up-left of the layer origin, the arms ending about 40 px right of and below it.
- **Rounded Bracket - double** [pass]: Two opposite rounded corner brackets (top-left, plus a bottom-right copy from a Repeater with 2 copies rotated 180 deg), each an L made by subtracting one 50 px rounded rectangle from another; orange fill, white stroke. A Point Control sets the spacing: Repeater Position = its point and the shape Position = point / -2 keep the pair centred.
- **Seaside Daisy** [pass]: A spiky daisy: one 8-point Polystar with extreme roundness (inner 640%, outer 660%) pinched by Pucker & Bloat (-121); it renders as 16 thin, pointed orange-yellow petals in two alternating lengths (about 112 px and 90 px from the centre) with a periwinkle round-joined edge radiating from a solid centre (the petals do not visibly loop or overlap).
- **Tri Cog** [pass]: A purple three-lobed trefoil/propeller (3-point Polystar with inner radius 120 > outer 40 and high roundness) over a 125x75 rectangle in the same group, 3 px white stroke. Only loosely cog-like; look at a frame before relying on it.

### Sound Effects

- **Blaster**: A synthetic sci-fi laser 'pew': three sine Tone frequencies (1000, 3630 and 230 Hz) sweep linearly down to 0 Hz over 0.30-0.37 s. Reverb is applied but set to Reverb Time 0, Wet Out 0, Dry Out 291%, so it adds no tail, only gain.
- **Busy**: The North American busy signal: Tone at 480 + 620 Hz with Level held on/off four times (about 0.47 s on, 0.53 s off), 'beep-beep-beep-beep' over 3.47 s.
- **CallAdobe**: Touch-tone (DTMF) dialling of 1-408-536-6000 (Adobe's San Jose number): 11 dual-frequency key tones of about 0.27 s each over 5.87 s, made with hold keys on Tone Frequency 1 and 2.
- **DTMF**: Touch-tone keypad beeps dialling 1-2-3-4-5-6-7-8-9-0 in order (correct DTMF frequency pairs, about 0.27 s per key with 0.5 s gaps) over 6.91 s.
- **PhoneCompany**: Machine speed-dialling: the same number as CallAdobe (1-408-536-6000) dialled twice in 2 s with very short (about 0.04 s) DTMF pulses on all five Tone frequencies; Frequency 5 repeats the pattern about 0.07 s late, so the tones overlap and chatter.
- **RingingPhone-US**: The North American ringback tone (440 + 480 Hz, what a caller hears while the other phone rings): Level on 2 s, off 4 s, on 2 s, 8 s in total.

### Synthetics

- **Blue Bars** [pass]: An animated blue bar background: Fractal Noise chunked by Mosaic into 15 vertical bars (15 x 1 blocks), coloured blue/navy by Tritone, then scaled 200% and rotated by the Transform effect so the bars tilt and swing from +25 to -25 deg while their brightness shimmers as the noise evolves.
- **CD Back** [pass]: A CD seen from the back: a silver disc as tall as the layer (outside it the layer is transparent) with a narrow rainbow prism band through the centre, built from noise and a ramp wrapped by Polar Coordinates and coloured by Colorama; an Angle Control spins the band round the disc. Not concentric rainbow rings and not a full-frame fill.
- **Cells** [pass]: An organic purple-blue cellular texture: an inverted Bubbles Cell Pattern smoothed by Median, coloured by Levels and Tritone, and distorted by Ripple from the centre; the cells slowly evolve.
- **Chip Foil** [pass]: Fills the layer with shimmering metallic foil flakes: Cell Pattern and Fractal Noise coloured by Colorama, light shifted by a 'Faux Light Angle' control, cut back to the layer's own shape with Set Matte.
- **Gold Ambiance** [pass]: A warm golden 4-Color Gradient background (pale yellow, gold, amber and brown points) balanced with Levels; the four colour points sweep diagonally across the frame and swap corners over 5 s.
- **Holographic Foil** [pass]: Fills the layer with a violet liquid-metal foil. Effect stack in order: Fractal Noise, Bevel Alpha (the bevelled frame edge), a second Fractal Noise, Directional Blur streaks, Gaussian softening, Colorama 'Rainbow Cast', then Tritone with violet midtones, which leaves only violet, purple and lilac tones, and Set Matte to cut it back to the layer's shape. As rendered it is not multicolour rainbow.
- **Mosaic** [pass]: A steel-blue tile mosaic: Fractal Noise pixelated into a 10 x 10 grid by Mosaic (tiles take the layer's aspect, so 16:9 rectangles on a 16:9 solid) and coloured by Tritone, with tiles changing shade as the noise evolves.
- **Orange Streaks** [pass]: An animated warm texture of diagonal orange light streaks: Fractal Noise stretched by Directional Blur (128 deg, length = half the layer height by expression), shaped by two Levels and coloured orange by Tritone. Evolution runs on the expression time*135, so it keeps churning.
- **Smoke - Drifting** [pass]: Drifting teal smoke: horizontally stretched Fractal Noise (Scale Width 1000, Height 200) given relief by Color Emboss, enlarged slightly by Transform (125 x 110%) and tinted teal by Tritone and Levels; it churns as Evolution runs and the noise slowly rotates.
- **Starburst Spin** [pass]: Blue star-shaped outlines (Radio Waves polygon, 10 sides, Star on) emitted from the layer centre at 1 wave per second; each wave narrows from a 44 px to a 5 px stroke, spins and fades, so the frame gradually fills with rotating nested stars.
- **Topographic Lines** [pass]: A static contour-map pattern: large blurred Fractal Noise posterised into 16 bands, the band edges traced by Find Edges, softened and tinted to dark-green lines on white.

### Text/3D Text

- **3D Basic Position Z Cascade In** [pass]: Characters cascade in left to right: each starts transparent, 100 px above and 500 px toward the camera (Position 3D [0,-100,-500], Opacity 0) and settles into place as a Ramp Up range sweeps across (Offset -50 -> 100).
- **3D Basic Position Z Type In** [pass]: Characters pop in one at a time, left to right, like a 3D typewriter: each starts transparent and 500 px toward the camera (Position 3D [0,0,-500], Opacity 0) and drops into place as the Range Selector Start sweeps 0 -> 100%.
- **3D Basic Rotate X Cascade In** [pass]: Characters cascade in left to right, each flipping 180 deg about its X axis while fading up from 0 opacity (Ramp Up range, Offset -25 -> 100).
- **3D Basic Rotate Y Cascade In** [pass]: Characters cascade in left to right, each swinging in from -120 deg about its Y axis (like doors) while fading up from 0 opacity.
- **3D Bouncing In Centered** [pass]: Characters tumble in left to right (Rotation X 360, Y 90 and Z 90 while fading from 0 opacity) around a word-grouped pivot whose Grouping Alignment slides from [200%, -50%] to [0%, -50%], so each letter swings in on an arc. There are no literal bounce keyframes; the 'bounce' is the arcing pivot.
- **3D Fall Back Scale & Skew In** [pass]: Characters start transparent, scaled 1200%, skewed 25 deg and placed 250 px below and 500 px toward the camera, then fall back to their normal size and shape in a left-to-right cascade; a Wiggly Selector (amount 100 -> 0 over 2 s) staggers them irregularly, and a second animator fades them up.
- **3D Fall Back Scramble & Blur In** [pass]: Three animators: characters fall back from 1200% scale, 250 px below and 500 px toward the camera (cascade with a wiggly stagger); a second animator scrambles them (Character Offset 10) and blurs them (Blur 30) until its amount reaches 0 at 1.67 s; a third fades them up. The real text has resolved at 2 s.
- **3D Flip In Rotate X** [pass]: Characters flip in left to right: each rotates 180 deg about X around a pivot 100 px above and 50 px in front of the letter (Anchor Point 3D [0,-100,-50]), swinging over into place while fading up from 0 opacity.
- **3D Flip Out Rotate X** [pass]: Exit move: left to right, each character rotates 180 deg about X around a pivot 100 px above it and fades to 0 opacity (Ramp Down range, Offset -25 -> 100).
- **3D Flip Up Reflection** [pass]: Characters flip up one at a time, left to right (Range Selector Start 0 -> 100%): before their turn they lie tipped back 100 deg on X, stretched to 300% height, blurred 10 px and at 80% opacity, so the line first shows as a smeared floor reflection, then each letter rises upright.
- **3D Flutter In From Left** [pass]: Characters fade in while falling from about 300-500 px above and tumbling in 3D (random rotation, scale and position jitter from a Wiggly selector, plus blur), settling into place in a wave that runs left to right.
- **3D Flutter In Random Order** [pass]: Characters drop in from above in random order, starting 3x larger, heavily blurred and transparent, tumbling with random 3D rotation before snapping into place.
- **3D Flutter Out From Right** [pass]: Exit animation: characters tumble away in 3D (random rotation, scale and position jitter, blur), dropping 300-500 px down and fading out, starting from the right end of the line.
- **3D Fly In & Unfold** [pass]: All characters animate at once: the line starts pulled toward camera (300 % scale, Z -300, tracking -40, spun 360 degrees on Y, blurred and transparent) and unfolds into its normal layout as Max Amount falls to 0; the Round selector shape makes the middle characters move most, and a Wiggly selector adds a vertical/depth scatter that also settles to 0.
- **3D Fly In From Behind Camera** [pass]: Characters start 1000 px towards the viewer at 5x scale, transparent and blurred, then fly back into place one after another from left to right.
- **3D Fly In Random & Rotate Y** [pass]: Characters fly in from in front of the text in random order, 4x larger, spinning a full turn on the Y axis and fading in.
- **3D Rain In By Word** [pass]: Words fall in from above and in front of the text, tumbling on X, Y and Z and fading in, one word after another from left to right.
- **3D Random Spike Tumble In** [pass]: Characters in random order start as tall vertical spikes (stretched 8x on Y, vertical blur 200, transparent) spinning a full turn on X, and collapse into normal letters; they pass through a yellow fill (#FFD200) while landing.
- **3D Random Tumble In By Word** [pass]: Words in random order tumble forward from 500 px behind the text, rotating a full turn on X while fading in.
- **3D Resolve Position In** [pass]: Characters start scattered at random X (up to +/-200 px) and depth (up to +/-300 px) offsets, blurred and transparent, and resolve into their correct places in a left-to-right wave.
- **3D Rotate in by Character** [pass]: Characters swing in rotating 180 degrees on the Y axis from 400 px behind the text at half height, fading in one after another from left to right.
- **3D Rotate in by Character X** [pass]: Characters flip up into place rotating on the X axis from -90 degrees around a pivot behind them, like a rolling split-flap board; rigged with expression controls.
- **3D Rotate out by Character X** [pass]: Exit animation: characters flip away on the X axis around a pivot behind them, like a split-flap board rolling over; rigged with expression controls.
- **3D Rotate out by Word** [pass]: Exit animation: words spin a full turn on Y while rising and moving toward camera, squashing to half height and fading out, one word after another from left to right.
- **3D Scramble in Position Z** [pass]: Characters arrive from deep in Z at random depths with scrambled letters (character offset) that settle to the real text while fading in, in random order.
- **3D Spiral Down & Unfold** [pass]: Each character spins three full turns on Y around a pivot 100 px behind it while dropping from 300 px above, starting at 50 % opacity, stretched 2x vertically and blurred; tracking opens from -31 to normal as it lands. All characters move at once and the Round selector shape makes the middle of the line spin hardest.
- **3D Spiral Rotate In by Line** [pass]: Characters rotate in (Y -200, X 25 degrees) around their line's anchor point from 200 px above and 500 px behind, in a left-to-right wave, while tracking opens from -50 and blur clears; they start at 25 % opacity, so the text is faintly visible before the wave reaches it.
- **3D Spiral Rotate Out by Line** [pass]: Exit animation: characters spin a full turn on Y around their line's anchor, drop 200 px and move 500 px back, spreading their tracking (+50) and blurring as they fade out, starting from the end of the text.
- **3D Swing In On Path** [pass]: Adds a mask to the text layer, puts the text on that path and slides it along (First Margin) while characters swing in on X and Y and fade up.
- **3D Twist & color characters** [pass]: A wave passes through the text that flips each character a full turn on X and recolours it to a pink-red fill (about #E62E4D); the text stays visible throughout and ends recoloured.

### Text/Animate In

- **Alternating Characters In** [pass]: Odd and even characters slide in from opposite vertical directions and scale up into place, like a zipper closing; rigged with expression controls.
- **Blinking Cursor Typewriter Console** [pass]: Types the text on character by character with a blinking cursor glyph at the end, like a computer terminal; Source Text is driven by an expression.
- **Center Spiral In** [pass]: Characters swirl into place: with Anchor Point Grouping set to All, each character orbits the centre of the whole text block while the rotation unwinds from 1440 degrees (four turns) to 0 over 2.5 s; a Triangle selector gives the middle characters the full spin and the end characters little, so the line uncurls from a spiral. A second animator fades the whole text up over the first 1.43 s.
- **Characters Shuffle In** [pass]: Characters slide in horizontally from about 670 px to the left one after another, overlapping as they pass so the letters seem to shuffle into place.
- **Decode In By Random Character** [pass]: Characters appear in random order as flickering random glyphs that decode into the real text (Character Value with a Full Unicode range, scrambled by a Wiggly selector at 2 per second); a Posterize Time effect at 20 fps makes it choppy like a digital readout, and the layer itself fades in over the first 0.5 s.
- **Decoder Fade In** [pass]: Characters fade in from left to right while the letters at the reveal edge cycle through other characters before settling, a soft decoder look.
- **Drop In By Character** [pass]: Characters drop from about 290 px above into their places one after another from left to right (no overshoot or bounce, no fade).
- **Espresso Eye Chart** [pass]: Characters start small (44 %), faint (25 %) and scrambled into other letters, then grow, brighten and resolve to the real text in random order.
- **Fade Up And Flip** [pass]: Words fade in while flipping from upside-down and stretched (vertical scale -200 %) to normal, one word after another.
- **Fade Up Characters** [pass]: Characters fade in one after another from left to right, a soft typewriter-like reveal.
- **Fade Up Lines** [pass]: Each line of the text fades in one after another from top to bottom.
- **Fade Up Words** [pass]: Words fade in one after another from left to right.
- **Fly In From Bottom** [pass]: Characters fly in diagonally from about 240 px right and 295 px below into place, one after another from left to right.
- **Fly In With A Twist** [pass]: Characters fly up from about 285 px below (and 50 px right) while spinning a full turn, landing one after another from left to right, starting straight away at comp.time.
- **Opacity Flicker In** [pass]: Characters switch on in random order within 0.44 s, then the text flickers like a faulty sign or a screen powering up: the layer's own Opacity stutters around +0.7 s, most characters blank out for a moment around +1.06 s, more hold-key flicker follows to +1.5 s and a last dip at +2.5 s.
- **Pop Buzz Words** [pass]: Replaces the layer's text with Source Text hold keyframes that flash one word at a time every 0.5 s (sample words 'Pop', 'Buzz', 'Words', 'Every', 'Fifteen', 'Frames' in Myriad Pro Bold / Bold Italic).
- **Raining Characters In** [pass]: Characters fall from about 340 px above in random order, cycling through random glyphs as they drop and fade in, with an Echo effect leaving motion trails.
- **Random Fade In** [pass]: Characters fade in individually in random order until the whole text is visible.
- **Random Shuffle In** [pass]: Characters slide in horizontally from about 670 px to the left in random order, crossing each other so the text appears to shuffle into place.
- **Random Word Shuffle In** [pass]: Words slide in horizontally from about 670 px to the left in random order and shuffle into their places.
- **Slide Down By Character** [pass]: Each character drops about 73 px from above into its final position while fading on, cascading left to right in a soft ramp over about 1.5 s.
- **Slide In By Line** [pass]: Each line slides in from the left (about 360 px offset) while fading on, top line first, staggered line by line over about 1 s.
- **Slide In From Comp Edge** [pass]: Words (preset default; or characters via 'Animate By') slide in one after another from fully outside a chosen comp edge (default Top; menu Top, Bottom, Left, Right) to their resting place in 1.33 s, with no fade. A Position expression measures the text and comp so the start is always just off-frame.
- **Slide Up By Character** [pass]: Characters shoot up from far below the frame (7445 px offset at full selection, strongly eased) and decelerate into place one after another while fading on, left to right over about 2 s.
- **Slide Up By Word** [pass]: Each word shoots up from far below the frame (7445 px offset at full selection, strongly eased) and decelerates into place while fading on, word by word over about 2 s; a clean caption-style entrance.
- **Slide and Pop In** [pass]: Each word (preset default; the 'Animate By' menu can switch to characters) pops on with a hard opacity cut about 94 px ABOVE its final spot, then slides down into place with an eased settle; the reveal sweeps left to right and lands in 1 s. There is no bounce or overshoot: the 'pop' is the instant appearance.
- **Slow Fade On** [pass]: Characters fade on gradually in a soft wave across the text over about 2 s, so the line appears gently rather than all at once.
- **Smooth Move In** [pass]: Starts at once: the whole line fades up over the first 0.5 s while characters arrive as huge (up to 400%), heavily skewed (70 deg), semi-transparent glyphs piled up near the line's left end, then shrink, unskew and settle into place in a left-to-right ramp that finishes at +2 s: a smooth cinematic slam-down of oversized letters.
- **Spin In By Character** [pass]: Each character in turn (left to right) spins 10 full turns while travelling from about 242 px right / 295 px down into its place, over 2 s. There is no fade: before and at the start the whole text is visible, shifted down-right.
- **Spin In By Word** [pass]: Each word, anchored as a unit (Anchor Point Grouping = Word), spins 2 full turns while travelling from about 242 px right / 295 px down into place, word by word over 1.5 s. No fade: the text is visible, displaced, at the start.
- **Straight In By Character** [pass]: Characters slide straight in from the right (about 636 px offset) to their places one after another, left to right, over 3.5 s. No fade: before the move the whole line sits visible 636 px to the right.
- **Straight In By Word** [pass]: Words slide straight in from the right (about 617 px offset) to their places one after another, left to right, over 1.33 s. No fade: before the move the whole line sits visible 617 px to the right.
- **Straight In Multi-Line** [pass]: Lines slide in horizontally from the right (about 574 px offset) one after another, top line first, building the block over 2.5 s (Range Start sweep, 50% smoothness). No fade: lines wait visible to the right until their turn.
- **Straight In Multi-Line 2** [pass]: Lines slide in horizontally from the right (about 592 px offset) in an overlapping eased wave (Range Offset -50 -> 100 with a 50% Ramp Up window), top line first, over 2 s. No fade: lines wait visible to the right until their turn.
- **Stretch In Each Line** [pass]: Characters grow from 0% scale relative to their line's anchor (Anchor Point Grouping = Line, aligned toward the line's right end), so each line stretches open out of a point while a soft ramp sweeps left to right over 2.5 s. No fade and no tracking change.
- **Stretch In Each Word** [pass]: Characters grow from 0% scale relative to their word's anchor (Anchor Point Grouping = Word, aligned toward the word's right end), so each word stretches open out of a point while a soft ramp sweeps left to right over 2.5 s. No fade and no tracking change.
- **Twirl On Each Line** [pass]: Characters grow from 0% scale, spin 4 full turns around their line's left end (Anchor Point Grouping = Line) and fade on, in a soft left-to-right ramp over about 3.5 s.
- **Twirl On Each Word** [pass]: Characters grow from 0% scale, spin 4 full turns around their word's left end (Anchor Point Grouping = Word) and fade on, in a soft left-to-right ramp over about 3.5 s.
- **Typewriter** [pass]: Characters appear one at a time from left to right with no fade, like text being typed, over 2.5 s.
- **Wipe In To Center** [pass]: Characters reveal from both outer ends of the line toward the centre, meeting in the middle, in a quick 0.5 s move that starts at once (comp.time +0 to +0.5 s); by +0.5 s the full line is on.

### Text/Animate Out

- **Alternating Characters Out** [pass]: Characters split apart vertically - odd characters move up, even characters move down (Y Position Offset 100 px, via an Expression Selector) - while shrinking to nothing (Reveal menu 'Scale Down', default) or fading ('Fade Out'), sweeping left to right; done in 1.33 s.
- **Decode Out By Random Character** [pass]: Characters, in random order, turn into random glyphs (Wiggly selector on Character Value, Full Unicode range; some come out blank or as punctuation) and dim as the Range Selector sweeps over +0 to +1.33 s. They do not vanish one by one: the scrambled line stays on screen until the preset's own Transform Opacity fade 100 -> 0% (+0.87 s to +1.62 s) takes it off. Posterize Time at 20 fps (renamed 'Decode Out Speed') makes the scramble step.
- **Encoder Fade Out** [pass]: Starting at once, a hard-edged boundary sweeps left to right: each character cycles through the next ~19 characters of its code range (Character Offset 19) as it fades out, finishing at +3.47 s: a slow digital 'encoding' exit.
- **Fade Out By Character** [pass]: Characters fade out one after another from left to right over about 2 s.
- **Fade Out Slow** [pass]: The text fades out slowly and softly in a wave across the characters over about 2 s.
- **Opacity Flicker Out** [pass]: The layer's own Transform Opacity flickers on hold keyframes (on/off bursts at +0 to +0.17 s and +0.54 to +0.83 s) while characters switch off in random order from +0.42 s to +1.42 s, after a brief whole-text blink at +0.4 s; like a failing sign. Text is gone by about +1.42 s; the opacity keys end at +1.96 s.
- **Raining Characters Out** [pass]: Starting at once, characters in random order drop 340 px, fade to 1% opacity and scramble into other glyphs (Character Offset 44) as a ramp sweeps through until +2.97 s; Echo (0.25 s, about 4 echoes, decay 0.5, Add) stacks ghost copies so they streak like rain.
- **Random Fly Off** [pass]: Characters, in random order, slide 650 px to the right (no rotation, no fade) from comp.time +0 s to +2.47 s. They do not disappear: the text ends parked 650 px right unless that is off-frame.
- **Random Word Fly Off** [pass]: Words, in random order, slide 578 px to the right with strong easing (no rotation, no fade) from comp.time +0 s to +1 s. They do not disappear: the text ends parked 578 px right unless that is off-frame (a centred title leaves its first letters at the right edge).
- **Slide Off Right By Character** [pass]: Characters slide 848 px to the right one after another starting from the LAST character (right to left, so the line peels away from its right end), from comp.time +0 s to +3.47 s. No fade: anything still inside the frame after the move stays visible.
- **Slide Off Right By Word** [pass]: Words slide 848 px to the right one after another starting from the LAST word (right to left), from comp.time +0 s to +1.5 s. No fade: anything still inside the frame after the move stays visible.
- **Slide Out By Line** [pass]: Lines slide out to the right (about 360 px) while fading, bottom line first, staggered over 1 s.
- **Slide Out To Comp Edge** [pass]: Words (preset default; or characters via 'Animate By') slide one after another from their position to fully past a chosen comp edge (default Bottom; menu Top, Bottom, Left, Right) in 1.33 s, with no fade; a Position expression measures text and comp so the end is just off-frame.
- **Slide and Pop Out** [pass]: Each word (preset default; or characters via 'Animate By') slides up about 94 px and then snaps off with a hard opacity cut at the top of its move, sweeping left to right within 1 s; the mirror of Slide and Pop In.
