The AE gallery comp was 0.4 s long on a layer starting at source 1.5 s, and the PR card found flashes are tied to clip time 0, so the failure is most likely the harness, not the effect. It should be 'needs re-test', not unusable.
- pr_effect 'Camera Shake' (Film Impact): verdict pass from one still frame that only shows a 1.8x push-in, and the card admits the shake needs playback. Recipes 'Impact shake hit' and 'Bass drop slam' then measured a smooth push and drift with no frame-to-frame jitter and replaced the effect. Camera Mode labels, which Strafe/Stride/Roll set belongs to which mode, and the meaning of 'Scale' are all unknown, so its use_for (short jolt with Master keyed to 0) is unproven. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: mode m reads the (m+1)-th Strafe / Stride / Roll set; modes 0-1 move under 1 px at 1920, modes 2-4 shake 8-44 px rms, which explains the smooth recipe renders at the default mode 1; judged pass on the jitter measurement. 'Scale' is still unexplained.)
- pr_audio 'Channel Mixer': the harvest row belongs to the clip's Volume component (match 'Internal Volume Mono'), params are empty and confidence is low, so there is effectively no card. Eleven more pr_audio cards have no params or low confidence (Distortion, Dynamics Processing, FFT Filter, Balance, Invert, Swap Channels, Fill Left with Right, Fill Right with Left, Channel Volume, Scientific Filter, Stereo Expander). Most audio mappings from 0..1 values to dB or LUFS are known only at the default (e.g. Loudness Meter target '-23 LUFS probably', Graphic EQ Range 'inferred'). (2026-10-14, live wave 3: resolved. The seven stereo-only effects now have their real rows from a stereo dump (Channel Mixer 65 named routing params, bipolar 0 = -100 %, 0.5 = 0 %, 1 = +100 %; Balance, Channel Volume, Stereo Expander, Swap / Fill measured), the batch rows replaced; 69 measured parameter mappings (dB / Hz / s / LUFS) sit in the audio cards' params[].measured, Loudness Meter's Target Level makes no audible change, Graphic EQ Range +/-24 dB measured.)
- ae_effect 'CC Radial Blur': status current with verdict wrong, and enum labels empty. Of 7 Types only Type 6 (spin) is verified, so the zoom type, which is the common use (rush/zoom punch), cannot be chosen. The labels can be read from the CycoreFX binary, as was done for CC Flo Motion. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: Type is 1 Straight Zoom, 2 Fading Zoom, 3 Centered Zoom, 4 separator, 5 Rotate, 6 Scratch, 7 Rotate Fading; the card says so and points the zoom punch at Types 1-2. Its rendered demo stays Type 6, so the zoom Types are known by label, not yet by frame.)
- tool 'Text animators and the Range Selector': marked high confidence, but the dropdown orders (Based On, Shape, Mode, Units) are unverified, the property list is partial, and Path Options and More Options are absent (see gaps). (2026-10-14, live wave 3: Based On / Shape / Mode / Units and the More Options menus read back from After Effects 26.0 and written into the text animator cards, with the display-name pairings they confirm.)
- tool 'Cameras (including depth of field)': too thin for camera moves (see gaps). Its claim that Advanced 3D depth of field needs 26.3+ is worth one test render on the installed 26.0: if DOF already renders there, the card blocks a usable feature. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: on 26.0 depth of field renders under Classic 3D (a layer 900 px off focus lost a third of its sharpness) and Advanced 3D hides the DOF properties; the card no longer blocks the feature.)
- Duplicate cards for the same item, with different demo settings and use text: pr_effect 'Transform' x2 and 'Noise' x2 (same match name), pr_transition 'Cross Dissolve (Legacy)' / 'Dip to Black (Legacy)' / 'Dip to White (Legacy)' x2, tool 'Adjustment layers' x2 (confidence low vs medium), recipe 'Glitch cut' x2 (impacts pass vs transitions not_rendered), ae_preset 'Spin In' x2 (two folders). pve.card() returns whichever file loads first.
- pr_effect 'Track Matte Key': a core technique that has never been rendered. Whether the 'Matte:' track index is 0- or 1-based is unverified, the harvested range is broken, and Reverse cannot be addressed by name. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: 'Matte:' is 1-based, 0 / default = none, Composite Using 0 Alpha / 1 Luma; rendered and judged pass; pve check ignores the broken uint32 range. Reverse is still an unnamed row.)
- Keyer cards (pr_effect Ultra Key, ae_effect Keylight (1.2), Linear Color Key, Color Range; recipe 'Green screen key' weak): every pass comes from keying a beige wall or navy areas on footage with no chroma screen. Nothing shows edge, hair or spill quality, which is what these cards are chosen for. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: a synthetic green-screen plate with fine strands and rim spill keyed cleanly with the Green screen key recipe (both apps) and the Keylight preset stack; Matte Choker and Refine Matte pass after Keylight; the Advanced Spill Suppressor Ultra demo greyed the whole frame there too (now Standard, not rendered). Ultra Key, Linear Color Key, Color Range, Chroma Key and Color Key were not re-rendered on it.)
- Film Impact transitions (e.g. 'Soft Wipe', 'Film Dissolve', 'Chaos'): direction, angle, feather and easing controls are 'not harvested', and the Premiere scripting gap list says transition parameters cannot be set. The edit system therefore gets defaults only, yet several cards present direction as a choice.
- ae_effect 'CC Force Motion Blur': the pass uses Shutter Angle 3600 (10 frames of cross-frame ghosting), far outside the card's own typical 180-720. It shows frame averaging on footage, not motion blur on an animated precomp, which is the card's stated main use.
- Recipe families text_motion (all 17) and transitions (12 of 19) are not_rendered, yet 10 of the 17 text recipes and 5 of the 12 unrendered transition recipes say confidence high. Timing claims such as 'letters land 0.3-0.5 s after the cue' are unmeasured.
- tool 'Type tool, graphics layers and text/track styles': thin. It has no Responsive Design - Position pinning, no Roll/Crawl, and no way to apply a Text/Track Style from a script. The only text route is .mogrt, which these cards should say plainly.
- tool 'Audio clip Volume and fades': the Channel Volume and Panner param names were never harvested, so clip panning has no scripted path. A small inconsistency too: fade keys at 10^(-90/20) are about -75 dB on the card's own scale, not -90. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: fixed: the fade key 10^(-90/20) is -75 dB on the Level scale (inaudible either way), Volume Level measured, Channel Volume rows are Left / Right on the same scale.)
- ae_effect 'Turbulent Noise': the Noise Type and Overflow labels are 'assumed identical to Fractal Noise' and the Blending Mode labels beyond 2 are unverified. This is typical of the roughly 165 hedged AE effect cards that a label harvest would fix in bulk. (2026-10-14, live wave 3: resolved in bulk: its Noise Type / Overflow / Blending Mode menus are read from its own plug-in, and harvest_enums.hedged() finds no AE effect card hedging on labels any more.)

### Doubtful

- Stale verdicts after fixes: 18 'wrong' and 10 'weak' recipes have rerender:true. Their values were changed after the failed render and never rendered again, so neither the old verdict nor the new values are verified. Examples: 'Paparazzi strobe' (probability 50 to 6, still random per frame and could still burst), 'Impact shake hit' and 'Bass drop slam' (Camera Shake swapped for a keyed Transform jitter that has never been seen), the 'Grungy film reel' / 'Black-and-white newsreel' / 'Silent-era film' strobes, 'Film burn cut' and 'Title disintegrates to dust' (end keys moved to -0.04).
- 'wrong' marks corrected cards: 53 current AE presets (plus 14 obsolete) are 'wrong' with rerender:false because the card text was rewritten to match the render (mostly 'starts at comp.time, no hold'). The card is probably right now, but PVE.md tags it [wrong]. The verdict needs a 'corrected' state, or a second render, so a consumer can tell 'was wrong, now fixed' from 'is wrong'.
- 'broken' used for harness limits rather than effect faults: 13 VR effects (aerender on the CPU renderer showed a 'requires GPU acceleration' slate), Warp Stabilizer and 3D Camera Tracker (rendered before background analysis finished), and Matte Choker, CC RepeTile, Separate XYZ Position, CC Composite, Apply Color LUT, CC Time Blend (FX) (the single-layer demo cannot exercise them). These should read 'untested: <cause>'. The VR set can be re-rendered with GPU acceleration on.
- 'pass' means the card text matched the render, not that the effect works: e.g. pr_transition 'Stretch' (Film Impact) is status unusable with verdict pass, and 'Morph Cut' passes by showing its failure case. The PVE.md legend reads [v] as 'matched its render', which a planner can mistake for 'works'. Suppress [v] on non-current cards, or define it in the legend.
- Every 'both' recipe is verified on the Premiere side only: gallery.py recipe_gallery renders only the 'premiere' stack. The 'after_effects' halves (including stacks with two of the same effect, e.g. two Wave Warp in the archive looks and two Glow in 'Glint sparkle') have never been rendered, yet the recipe-level 'pass' covers them. Separately, Premiere builds dropped the values of a second Strobe Light on one clip. The recipes worked around it by removing the effect, so the builder bug with two instances of one effect on one clip is probably still there. (2026-10-14, live wave 2: every After Effects half is now rendered and judged in verified.ae and the second-instance controls pass; see next_steps.)
- Keying passes on footage with no chroma screen (Ultra Key, Keylight, Linear Color Key, Color Range, Chroma Key, Color Key): each verdict proves only that some colour went transparent. (2026-10-14: partly settled by the synthetic green-screen plate, see weak_cards 'Keyer cards'; the Premiere and the simpler AE keyers still rest on the old renders.)
- Temporal effects were verified on a source described two ways: the Camera-Shake Deblur note says test_a.mp4 is locked off with zero frame-to-frame shift, while the Pixel Motion Blur and CC Wide Time notes say the clip cuts between camera angles every few frames. Passes for Echo, CC Wide Time, Pixel Motion Blur, Time Difference and Time Displacement may be measuring ghosting across cuts rather than motion. Document the gallery source once and re-check these.
- Cleanup recipes and cards whose demo source has no defect (Rolling Shutter Repair, 'Phone pan jello fix', 'Interlace combing...', 'Frame-rate judder conform', 'Low-res archive upscale cleanup', Camera-Shake Deblur): they are weak or broken because nothing was there to fix. The verdicts are honest, but these tools are still unverified for their real job. (2026-10-14, live wave 3 on the second footage set: Rolling Shutter Repair (both apps) and Phone pan jello fix pass, removing Rate % of one frame of pan skew; Wire removal and Archive dust pass; Camera-Shake Deblur, Reduce DV blockiness and Low-res archive upscale cleanup are weak; Frame-rate judder conform is broken (Timewarp at 100 % blends nothing on a 30-to-25 VFR clip); the interlace fix stays untested because both apps field-separate a flagged 1080i file on import.)
- pr_effect 'Camera Shake' pass: a single still cannot show a shake, and later playback measurements contradict the card (see weak_cards). (2026-10-14: resolved by the jitter measurement, see weak_cards.)
- pr_effect 'Strobe Light' pass: the frame check is fine, but the inverted Duration rule and the per-period probability it derived from that render conflict with the recipe measurement and with Adobe's documentation (see weak_cards).
- ae_effect 'Strobe Light' unusable: contradicted by the same plug-in working in Premiere (see weak_cards).
- No verification at all for 379 PR presets, 214 mogrts and 53 PR audio effects, and the text recipes depend on the mogrts. LUT cards are better supported because they were analysed from the .cube/.look data, but their summaries' look descriptions (e.g. 'skies turn grey-blue') were never compared against a rendered frame. (2026-10-14, live wave 2: 22 of the 214 templates are now rendered and timed, and the 17 text recipes judged.)
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 2): fixed after their render and NOT rendered again (corrected_unverified): Analogue broadcast tape; Chromatic pulse; Cinematic teal-orange; Cinematic tracking breathe; Colour splash; Colour to archive era shift; Crisis red edge; Damaged VHS tape; Decode scramble reveal; Floating dust motes; Gaming Lower Third Left; Glint sparkle on highlights; God rays from above; God-ray title; Gold light-sweep reveal; Light burst flash; Lightning strike flash; Low-light noise cleanup; Map location pulse ring; Music ducks under VO (scripted, from VO ranges); News lower-third tag; News ticker crawl (looping); Overshoot word slam; Quote card; RGB split hit; Rewind to the past (tape); Rising embers; Saffron colour splash; Shockwave thump; Speed ramp slam; Sports Intro; Sports Lower Third Side; Stat counter hit; Stutter step; Talking-head skin smoothing; Timelapse and archive deflicker; Title disintegrates to dust. The next live wave should re-render these first.
- ae_lib.jsx dress lets one property error end the rest of a layer's effect stack ('Colour to archive era shift' lost all five effects to one refused Hue/Saturation key), while pr_build.jsx catches per property. pve.check_effects now flags keys on properties the harvest marks not animatable (932 After Effects properties), but any other per-property error still drops the stack silently: catch per property in dress().
- Brightness & Contrast does not share a scale across the apps: at the same values Premiere renders the layout backplates 10-27 levels darker than After Effects. Any other recipe that copies Brightness & Contrast values between its premiere and after_effects stacks may differ the same way (unmeasured outside the layout recipes).
- Premiere snaps an audio-only clip's in point to the item's 25 fps media timebase (the .prproj stored 4.666667 s for 4.7 s). pr_build sets audio in points with ProjectItem.setInPoint in seconds and does not warn; any spec that stacks two copies of one file (lifts, doubled SFX) must keep their in points on a 0.2 s grid. Whether a WAV file gets the same timebase is unmeasured.
- The ticker fix measures one repeat as (3-copy ink width - 1-copy ink width) / 2 from a hidden CRAWL_UNIT layer; it assumes no kerning between repeats and the same tracking on both layers, and it has not been rendered.
- Text timing in the wave-2 build: templates hosted on the plain 'Fade' template never settle in a 4 s slot (Fade's in ends at 2.24 s, its out starts at 2.84 s of 5 s), so the five recipes judged on it show the host's motion more than their own; After Effects titles have no out preset and end on a cut.
- Devanagari through decode templates and 'Decoder Fade In' scrambles through invented clusters (virama half-forms) before it resolves: readable at the end, unreadable mid-animation.
- Most gap-recipe and AE-half verdicts rest on 2-4 sampled frames: staggers and sequences were checked with extra frames only for the bar, pie and parallax renders; the cut and flicker recipes whose keys end before 0.2 s are untested:needs_setup although their early render frames show them working.
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 3): the label harvest names every menu item, but a label is menu text, not a render: modes never rendered (CC Radial Blur's zoom Types, most blend / transfer modes, Strobe Operator 2-13, Pixel Motion Blur's Manual shutter) are known by name only. Pixel Motion Blur's gallery demo ran on Shutter Control 2 (Automatic), so its Shutter Angle 720 never applied; its verdict describes the comp's own shutter.
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 3): the second footage set is synthetic. The rolling-shutter plate skews 4.8 frame-times of pan motion, more than a real sensor can, so the repairs removed only the physically possible share; the shake plate's blur direction is random, which may be why Camera-Shake Deblur restored only patches; the Morph Cut face is a drawn oval, so face tracking on a real interview is unmeasured.
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 3): Frame-rate judder conform is broken as a technique (Timewarp Frame Mix at Speed 100 changes nothing on a 30-to-25 fps VFR clip in either app); the working routes (AE layer Frame Blending, Premiere Frame Blend interpolation, a CFR transcode before import) are not spec-settable in the bridge. The interlace fix cannot be shown while the bridge has no way to interpret a flagged file as progressive.
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 3): audio cards carry measurements (params[].measured, measured.defaults_on_tone) but no verdicts. Frequency readings are third-octave (Parametric Equalizer centre about 24 000 x v Hz, Bandpass / Notch / Simple Parametric EQ about 24 000 x v^2 Hz) and all tests ran at 48 kHz, so a sample-rate dependence is unmeasured; Parametric Equalizer's HP / LP cut-offs are assumed to share the band scale; Amplify's stereo Left / Right gain scale and Dynamics' Compressor Ratio scale beyond 0.5 are unmeasured.
- 2026-10-14 (live wave 3 verify): the wave report's highlight said Matte Choker left the alpha still's edge 'identical to the control', while its own edge arrays show the 24 px feather choked to about 8 px (the frames agree with the arrays): judge from the measurement arrays and frames, not a report's summary line.
- 2026-10-14: Advanced Spill Suppressor's demo is now Method 1 (Standard) after the Ultra demo greyed a green-screen plate (corrected_unverified, not rendered); Track Matte Key's Reverse row and Camera Shake's 'Scale' are still not addressable / explained.
